# SGW_Combat_Implementation_Master_v2

Markdown rendering of `SGW_Combat_Implementation_Master_v2.xlsx` from the SGW Claude Server Handoff Pack v1.2.
Generated by `xlsx_to_md.py`; cell values only (no formulas, formatting or comments).
Sheet order and headers are preserved verbatim. Evidence labels inside the
sheets control authority, per the pack's `docs/SOURCE_POLICY.md`.

## 00_README

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Purpose | Phase 1 weapon reconstruction audit. Original cooked client data is authoritative where available; uploaded legacy/fan-dev SQL is comparison/reference only. |
| Source hierarchy | 1) CookedDataItems/Abilities/Effects from recovered SGW QA client; 2) direct client command/schema evidence; 3) legacy resources.sql / sgw(2).sql only as cross-check; 4) reconstruction decisions. |
| Important rule | Do NOT copy legacy ability trees, ability sets, ammo costs, clip sizes, ammo compatibility, prerequisites or armor stats as canon. |
| Weapon→Ability method | Primary binding is inferred from matching ORIGINAL item Moniker IDs to ORIGINAL weapon Auto-Attack Ability Moniker IDs. Legacy items_event_sets is only a cross-check. |
| Current result | 1073 physical weapon item variants / 214 unique physical weapon names across 15 families. |
| Legacy cross-check | Most legacy item-event rows agree, but 54 physical weapon variants conflict and are isolated in 09_Legacy_Conflicts. |
| Ammo rule | Ammo consumption shown in Original Ammo Cost comes from the Cooked Ability description, NOT legacy required_ammo. |
| Magazine rule | Legacy Clip Size and Legacy Ammo Types are hypotheses only until confirmed against stronger evidence/runtime. |
| Next implementation step | Review P0 Human weapon families first (SMG, Assault Rifle, Pistol, LMG/Minigun, Shotgun, Rifle), then implement finite ammo/reload plumbing, then special/faction weapons. |
| Out of scope for this pass | Final class ability trees/sets, final balancing, reserve-ammo economy, armor stats, final loot/vendor ammo rules. |

_10 data rows, 2 columns._

| P1 | Ammo toggles / darts | Original toggle abilities survive | Original cooked abilities; some effects missing | Reconstruct conditions/effects after base ammo works |
| P1 | Grenade launcher / flamethrower | Items survive but base ranged fire path is unresolved | Original items + weapon abilities | Trace dedicated fire abilities/projectiles |

| Weapon item clip capacity | resources.sql items.clip_size | LEGACY SERVER STRUCTURE | Server schema supports a per-weapon clip size; individual values require validation. |
| Weapon compatible ammo types | resources.sql items.ammo_types[] + default_ammo_type | LEGACY SERVER STRUCTURE | Server schema supports selectable ammo types; compatibility lists require validation. |
| Current loaded ammo | sgw(2).sql sgw_inventory.ammo | LEGACY SERVER STRUCTURE | Each inventory weapon instance can store an ammo count. |

## 11_Open_Gaps

| Priority | Gap | Current evidence | Next action |
|---|---|---|---|
| P0 | Which legacy clip_size values are correct for each weapon? | Legacy resources.sql only | Cross-check package/UI/tooltips/runtime or in-game behavior before implementation. |
| P0 | Reserve ammo pool and refill rules | Inventory has current ammo, GM refill exists; no final reserve model yet identified | Trace server code/resources and client HUD/inventory behavior. |
| P0 | Exact ammo-type compatibility by weapon | Legacy ammo_types arrays + original ammo-toggle abilities | Cross-check item monikers, UI, ability conditions and runtime behavior. |
| P0 | Grenade launcher / flamethrower ranged firing path | Original items exist, but no base ranged Auto Attack moniker recovered | Trace weapon-specific abilities, item events, effects and animation/projectile hooks. |
| P0 | Which duplicate/dev/revision abilities are intended final | Cooked client contains MS/test/duplicate ability revisions | Prefer non-test/current naming only after cross-source validation. |
| P1 | Weapon modification/stat monikers | Original item monikers survive | Resolve moniker groups/effects before balancing. |
| P1 | Armor stats | Out of current weapon audit scope | Separate armor pass after weapons. |
| P1 | Ability trees / ability sets | Legacy fan-dev tables are incomplete/outdated | Design after weapon mechanics are stable; do not copy legacy trees. |

_8 data rows, 4 columns._

## 12_Sources

| Source | Authority | Use / Caution |
|---|---|---|
| Recovered SGW QA client — CookedDataItems | PRIMARY | Item IDs, names, descriptions, TechComp/tier, ranges, item monikers. |
| Recovered SGW QA client — CookedDataAbilities | PRIMARY | Weapon abilities, descriptions, cooldown/warmup, monikers, effect IDs. |
| Recovered SGW QA client — CookedDataEffects | PRIMARY | Effect names/descriptions linked from abilities. |
| resources.sql | LEGACY / FAN-DEV | Used only for item-event crosscheck, clip_size, ammo_types, visual component hypotheses. Not canonical. |
| sgw(2).sql | LEGACY / FAN-DEV | Inventory schema shows ammo-state architecture; not proof of final values/economy. |
| abilities names and ids(1).txt | CROSS-SOURCE EXTRACT | Human-readable ability list; useful for spot checks, not higher authority than cooked XML. |
| SlashCommands_FullTable(1).txt / random codes(1).txt | CLIENT/COMMAND EVIDENCE | GM commands including give ammo / infinite-ammo override support finite-ammo architecture. |
| User instruction | PROJECT RULE | Ability trees/sets and legacy combat values are not final; weapons must be reviewed independently before implementation. |

## 17_Ammo_Reload_Runtime

| Step | Required Behavior | Server Implementation | Primary Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Equip weapon | Weapon enters a bandolier slot. | Up to four weapon slots; F1–F4 activates one. | Tipps.txt Dialog 5863/5882 | SOURCE-BACKED |
| 2. Activate weapon | Set active_weapon_slot and active weapon template. | Weapon-gated abilities recompute enabled/disabled state. | Tipps.txt Dialog 5867/5882 | SOURCE-BACKED |
| 3. Auto attack start | Right-click hostile target starts basic auto attack loop. | Use the active weapon's source-backed base auto-attack ability. | Tipps.txt Dialog 5883 | SOURCE-BACKED |
| 4. Ammo check | Before an ammo-consuming attack, compare magazine to ability cost. | If current_magazine < cost: reject attack and signal reload required. | Tipps.txt + Cooked ability descriptions | SOURCE-BACKED behavior / server reconstruction |
| 5. Consume ammo | Successful attack subtracts cost. | current_magazine = current_magazine - ability_ammo_cost | Tipps.txt Dialog 5883 | SOURCE-BACKED behavior |
| 6. Empty | Weapon with insufficient loaded ammo cannot execute ammo-consuming attack. | Manual or automatic reload is required. | Tipps.txt Dialog 5883 | SOURCE-BACKED |
| 7. Manual reload | Reload button invokes Reload Ability 596 / Effect 658 path. | Transfer reserve into magazine up to capacity. | Tipps.txt + Cooked Ability 596 | SOURCE-BACKED + reconstruction transfer |
| 8. Auto reload on equip | If option enabled and clip not full, invoke reload after equip. | Do not reload if no compatible reserve ammo exists. | Tipps.txt Dialog 5883 | SOURCE-BACKED |
| 9. Auto reload when empty | If option enabled and loaded ammo reaches insufficient state, invoke reload. | Avoid reload loops when reserve=0. | Tipps.txt Dialog 5883 | SOURCE-BACKED |
| 10. Ammo-type switch | Toggle selected ammo/payload mode. | Only one mutually exclusive bullet/dart mode should be active at a time unless evidence proves stacking. | Cooked ammo-toggle abilities | SOURCE-BACKED toggle / reconstruction exclusivity |
| 11. Weapon switch | Switch F1–F4 active slot. | Persist magazine per weapon instance; recalc allowed abilities; optionally reload on equip. | Tipps.txt Dialog 5882/5883 | SOURCE-BACKED + reconstruction persistence |
| 12. GM override | Infinite-ammo flag bypasses consumption/empty checks. | Do not alter saved magazine/reserve values while override is enabled. | SlashCommands/random codes | CLIENT/COMMAND EVIDENCE + reconstruction |

## 22_Developer_Handoff

| Topic | Instruction |
|---|---|
| How to use this workbook | Start with 19_Dev_Queue. P0 establishes the minimum complete combat runtime. Then use 14_Combat_Implementation to wire each weapon template and mark progress. |
| Authority rule | Original cooked Item/Ability/Effect data and original tutorial/runtime evidence outrank resources.sql/sgw(2).sql. Legacy values never overwrite original evidence. |
| Weapon values | Columns prefixed Original are recovered evidence. Columns prefixed Legacy are reference-only. Columns prefixed Recon are project decisions and may be tuned. |
| Ammo implementation | Do not create an inventory-item economy first. Implement loaded magazine + reserve pool + reload + ammo mode state, then decide vendor/loot/restock rules separately. |
| Unknown resource weapons | Staff, Zat and Ribbon Device are intentionally not forced into the ballistic magazine system. Their resource/charge model stays TBD until focused review. |
| Grenade launcher warning | Current legacy/base mapping to HMG Auto Attack should not be treated as final launcher behavior. Dedicated launcher abilities/projectiles must be traced before release. |
| Armor warning | Do not assign '30% mitigation' to an entire armor family. Some specific item descriptions contain it; final armor formulas remain unresolved. |
| Ability trees | Do not copy legacy trees or sets. Build three-branch class trees later using the stable weapon/armor runtime and surviving original ability pool. |
| Definition of Done – weapon | Correct item/template + correct source-backed base attacks/effects + ammo/reload behavior + weapon-gated abilities + VFX/SFX where available + client UI feedback + in-game test. |
| Definition of Done – armor | Correct slot/equip behavior + stat apply/remove + source/reconstruction values documented + Character Sheet reflects changes + durability/repair if enabled + in-game test. |
| Cover is core combat | Do not balance weapon/ability damage in isolation. Resolve directional Low/Medium/High cover before final hit/damage and keep the same resolver for players and NPCs. |
| Cover numbers | Tier names/colors/direction are source-backed; exact numeric Low/Medium/High bonuses are not. Keep them tunable and explicitly RECONSTRUCTION until tested. |
| Ability audit rule | Every damaging/control ability must receive a Cover Interaction classification: direct directional, per-target cone, AoE/occlusion TBD, melee rule, or no cover interaction. |

## 25_Runtime_Pseudocode

| Routine | Reference Logic | Evidence / Caution |
|---|---|---|
| activate_weapon(slot) | assert slot in 0..3<br>weapon = bandolier[slot]<br>active_bandolier_slot = slot<br>recompute_weapon_gated_abilities(weapon)<br>if reload_on_equip and weapon.current_magazine < weapon.magazine_capacity:<br>    try_reload(weapon) | Bandolier/F1–F4 and reload-on-equip are source-backed; field/state layout is reconstruction. |
| can_execute_attack(weapon, ability) | assert weapon is active<br>assert weapon satisfies ability weapon requirement<br>cost = source_backed_ammo_cost(ability)<br>if infinite_ammo: return true<br>if cost > 0 and weapon.current_magazine < cost:<br>    return false<br>return true | Ammo use/empty behavior is source-backed. Exact server function is reconstruction. |
| execute_attack(weapon, ability, target) | if !can_execute_attack(...):<br>    if reload_when_empty: try_reload(weapon)<br>    return FAIL_NEEDS_AMMO<br>apply_original_ability_effects(ability, target)<br>if !infinite_ammo:<br>    weapon.current_magazine -= ammo_cost(ability)<br>return SUCCESS | Original Ability→Effect chain should remain authoritative where recovered. |
| try_reload(weapon) | needed = magazine_capacity - current_magazine<br>available = reserve_pool[weapon.ammo_pool_id]<br>transfer = min(needed, available)<br>if transfer <= 0: return FAIL_NO_RESERVE<br>begin_reload_timer(weapon.reload_seconds)<br>on_complete:<br>    weapon.current_magazine += transfer<br>    reserve_pool -= transfer | Reload requirement is source-backed; reserve transfer/pool model is reconstruction. |
| set_ammo_mode(weapon, mode) | assert mode in weapon.supported_ammo_modes<br>remove_other_mutually_exclusive_ammo_toggles()<br>weapon.selected_ammo_mode = mode<br>apply_surviving_original_toggle_semantics(mode) | Toggle behavior survives; mutual exclusivity and reserve accounting are reconstruction. |
| equip_armor(item, slot) | remove_stat_modifiers(previous_item)<br>equip(item, slot)<br>apply_stat_modifiers(item)<br>refresh_character_sheet_stats() | Equipment changing stats while equipped is source-backed; modifier storage/formula is reconstruction. |

## 26_Cover_System

| System Element | Behavior / Value | Evidence | Evidence Status | Implementation Rule | col6 | col7 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Cover tiers | None / Low / Medium / High | Original tutorial explicitly identifies Low, Medium and High cover. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | Implement exactly four runtime states including no cover. |  |  |
| Cover colors | Low=Orange; Medium=Yellow; High=Green | Original cover-indicator tutorial. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | Preserve for HUD feedback. |  |  |
| Directional cover | Cover protects from specific directions; HUD displays an arc indicating protected directions. | Original tutorial. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | Cover must be checked relative to incoming attack direction, not as a permanent global buff. |  |  |
| Positional gameplay | SGW is described as a ranged combat game relying heavily on battlefield position and effective use of cover. | Original tutorial. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | Cover evaluation belongs in the core attack resolver before final damage/effect application. |  |  |
| Crouch | Crouching slows movement and grants defensive benefits. | Original controls tutorial. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | Keep crouch as a separate defensive state; do not silently equate crouch with a cover tier. |  |  |
| Player use | Player receives cover state/indicator. | Original tutorial. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | Implement player cover detection and HUD state. |  |  |
| NPC/enemy use | Enemies also use/benefit from cover. | User-confirmed project requirement. | USER-CONFIRMED | Run the same cover-resolution rules for player and NPC defenders. |  |  |
| Cover stat interaction | Abilities such as Hunker Down reference Cover Defense. | Recovered ability data. | CONFIRMED CONCEPT / FORMULA UNRESOLVED | Expose cover_defense as a combat modifier hook; exact hit/defense formula remains reconstruction. |  |  |
| Numeric tier bonus | Exact Low/Medium/High defense bonuses are not recovered in current evidence. | No surviving authoritative formula/value yet. | MISSING / RECONSTRUCTION REQUIRED | Do not hardcode canon-looking percentages. Keep tuning values configurable. |  |  |
| Flanking | Attack from outside the protected arc should receive no cover-tier benefit. | Derived directly from directional-cover behavior. | RECONSTRUCTION STRONG | Use attack direction vs cover arc/normal to determine whether cover applies. |  |  |
| Cover geometry | World objects provide cover, but exact recovered node/volume representation has not yet been audited. | Tutorial confirms world objects; data representation unresolved. | PARTIAL / UNRESOLVED | Dev must audit existing map cover nodes/volumes before authoring new ones. |  |  |
| Cover destruction | No current source confirms destructible cover. | No supporting source in current dataset. | UNRESOLVED | Do not implement as original behavior without further evidence. |  |  |
|  |  |  |  |  |  |  |

## 27_Cover_Ability_Rules

| Ability Category | Examples | Cover Relevant? | Resolution Stage | Cover Method | Rule | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Basic ranged auto attack | Pistol / Rifle / SMG / LMG / HMG / Dart / Staff / Zat / Ribbon | YES | Before hit/damage resolution | Directional tier check | If attacker is inside protected arc, apply defender cover modifier; otherwise treat as no cover. | SOURCE-BACKED COVER + RECON RULE |
| Aimed/precision shot | Aimed Shot: Torso/Leg/Arm, Headshot | YES | Before hit/damage resolution | Directional tier check + ability accuracy modifier | Accuracy bonus does not automatically bypass cover unless an ability explicitly says it does. | SOURCE-BACKED ABILITY + RECON RULE |
| Cone fire | Shotgun Auto Attack, Cover Fire, Area Burst | YES per target | For each target | Directional cover check from attack origin to each target | Each target resolves cover independently. | RECONSTRUCTION STRONG |
| Suppression attack | Cover Fire / Suppression Shot | YES for damage/hit | Initial attack resolution | Cover check for hit/damage; suppression application semantics unresolved | Do not assume suppression penetrates cover merely because it is suppression. | PARTIAL / UNRESOLVED |
| Direct DoT application | Point Blank Shot and similar | YES for initial application | Initial hit only | Cover check on application | Once successfully applied, periodic ticks should not re-check current cover unless later evidence requires it. | RECONSTRUCTION PROVISIONAL |
| Ground-target AoE / grenade | Grenades, mortar, ground blasts | UNRESOLVED | Impact/explosion resolution | Need LOS/occlusion/cover policy | Do not apply normal direct-fire cover formula blindly; audit projectile/explosion behavior separately. | UNRESOLVED |
| Beam/channel direct target | Ribbon beams / channeled direct attacks | YES unless ability says otherwise | Initial and/or channel pulse | Directional cover check | Whether every pulse re-checks cover is unresolved; configurable server behavior required. | PARTIAL |
| Melee | Weapon melee / blade | UNRESOLVED / likely not standard cover | Contact resolution | Separate melee rule | Do not use ranged cover formula without evidence. | UNRESOLVED |
| Threat-only / taunt | Draw Fire / threat abilities | NO damage-cover check for pure threat component | Ability effect resolution | Threat effect separate from damage hit | If ability also deals damage, only its damage/hit component uses cover. | RECONSTRUCTION STRONG |
| Self/group buff | Aim, Heroism, Hunker Down, stances | NO | Buff application | No enemy-cover check | Cover Defense buffs modify defender state rather than being blocked by cover. | SOURCE-ALIGNED |
| Crowd control attached to ranged hit | Snare / Disarm / Knockdown / Fear / Disorient | YES via parent attack | After successful parent attack | Parent attack resolves cover first | Do not apply attached CC when the parent attack is rejected/misses unless the specific effect says otherwise. | RECONSTRUCTION STRONG |
| Ammo toggle | AP / Hollow Point / Incendiary / EMP / Explosive / Dart payloads | NO direct check | Modifier setup | Applied to subsequent attack resolution | Ammo mode changes attack/effect semantics; cover is still evaluated by the attack being fired. | SOURCE-ALIGNED / RECON |

## 28_Cover_Server_Schema

| Entity | Field | Type | Meaning | Evidence Status | Implementation Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| cover_definition | cover_id | PK | Unique cover point/segment/volume | RECONSTRUCTION SERVER FIELD | Actual map representation should reuse recovered engine/map cover data if it exists. |
| cover_definition | quality | ENUM | LOW / MEDIUM / HIGH | SOURCE-BACKED CONCEPT | Exact numeric modifier lives in tuning data. |
| cover_definition | facing_normal | VECTOR3 | Direction cover protects from | RECONSTRUCTION REPRESENTATION | Needed to implement directional cover. |
| cover_definition | protected_arc_degrees | FLOAT or arc endpoints | Directional protection arc | RECONSTRUCTION REPRESENTATION | HUD already communicates an arc; exact source geometry format unresolved. |
| cover_definition | world_ref | Actor/Node reference | Map object providing cover | SOURCE-BACKED CONCEPT | Prefer existing world/engine cover object rather than duplicate authored state. |
| actor_combat_state | active_cover_id | FK/NULL | Current cover source | RECONSTRUCTION SERVER FIELD | Same field model for players and NPCs. |
| actor_combat_state | cover_quality | ENUM | NONE/LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH | SOURCE-BACKED CONCEPT | Can be derived from active cover definition. |
| actor_combat_state | is_crouched | BOOL | true/false | SOURCE-BACKED BEHAVIOR | Crouch benefit remains separate from cover tier. |
| attack_resolution | incoming_direction | VECTOR3 | attacker → defender direction | RECONSTRUCTION SERVER FIELD | Used against cover facing/arc. |
| attack_resolution | cover_applies | BOOL | true/false | RECONSTRUCTION RESULT | False when no cover or attacker is outside protected arc. |
| attack_resolution | cover_tier | ENUM | NONE/LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH | SOURCE-BACKED CONCEPT | Used only when cover_applies=true. |
| attack_resolution | cover_defense_modifier | NUMERIC | Configurable by tier + buffs | RECONSTRUCTION REQUIRED | Do not hardcode source-looking values until formula is chosen. |
| attack_resolution | crouch_defense_modifier | NUMERIC | Configurable | RECONSTRUCTION REQUIRED | Separate from cover modifier so crouch can work outside cover. |
| attack_resolution | ability_accuracy_modifier | NUMERIC | e.g. Aimed Shot +Accuracy | ORIGINAL ABILITY WHERE PRESENT | Applied in same hit/defense resolution. |
| attack_resolution | final_hit_result | ENUM/BOOL | hit/miss/etc. | RECONSTRUCTION SERVER RESULT | Determined after accuracy/defense/cover calculation. |
| attack_resolution | final_focus_damage | NUMERIC | post-effect/mitigation | RECONSTRUCTION RESULT | Only after hit and defensive resolution. |
| attack_resolution | final_health_damage | NUMERIC | post-effect/mitigation | RECONSTRUCTION RESULT | Only after hit and defensive resolution. |
|  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  |  |  |  |  |  |
| Order | Combat Resolution Stage | Inputs | Rule |  |  |
| 1 | Acquire attack | Attacker, defender, active weapon, ability | Validate active weapon / ability / ammo first. |  |  |
| 2 | Resolve geometry | Attacker position, defender position, active cover geometry | Compute incoming attack direction. |  |  |
| 3 | Resolve directional cover | Cover normal/arc vs incoming direction | Return NONE/LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH active protection for this attack. |  |  |
| 4 | Resolve crouch | Defender crouch state | Apply separate crouch defensive contribution. |  |  |
| 5 | Resolve accuracy/defense | Ability accuracy + attacker ratings vs defender defense + cover defense + crouch | Exact mathematical formula remains reconstruction. |  |  |
| 6 | Hit result | Hit / miss / blocked-type result | If attack does not connect, do not apply ordinary damage/attached CC. |  |  |
| 7 | Resolve ability effects | Original Effect IDs and their Focus/Health/status payloads | Effect records remain primary source for base payload where recovered. |  |  |
| 8 | Resolve armor/resistance | Defender armor/resistance/mitigation | Final formulas still under reconstruction. |  |  |
| 9 | Apply Focus / Health | Final computed values | Keep Focus and Health separate. |  |  |
| 10 | Apply attached states | Suppression / stun / knockdown / snare / disarm / fear / DoT | Apply only according to parent-effect semantics. |  |  |
| 11 | Consume ammo | Ability ammo cost | Only for successfully executed attack attempt according to final server policy. |  |  |
| 12 | AI reaction | NPC target evaluates cover/reposition behavior | Player and enemy use same cover-quality logic; AI decision layer is separate. |  |  |

## 29_Cover_Test_Cases

| Test ID | System | Procedure | Expected Result | Evidence / Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| COV-001 | Tier Detection | Stand behind known Low cover facing attacker | Defender reports LOW; HUD orange; attack is considered protected from that direction. | SOURCE-BACKED tier/color |
| COV-002 | Tier Detection | Stand behind Medium cover | Defender reports MEDIUM; HUD yellow. | SOURCE-BACKED tier/color |
| COV-003 | Tier Detection | Stand behind High cover | Defender reports HIGH; HUD green. | SOURCE-BACKED tier/color |
| COV-004 | Direction | Stay in High cover; move attacker around behind defender | Cover applies only while attacker lies inside protected direction/arc. | SOURCE-BACKED directional cover |
| COV-005 | Flank | Attack same defender from unprotected side | No cover-tier modifier is applied. | RECONSTRUCTION from directional behavior |
| COV-006 | Player/NPC symmetry | Repeat identical shot against player and NPC using same High-cover setup | Both defenders use the same cover-resolution rules. | USER-CONFIRMED requirement |
| COV-007 | Crouch independent | Crouch without a cover object | Crouch defensive state can exist without falsely reporting Low/Medium/High cover. | SOURCE-BACKED crouch + reconstruction |
| COV-008 | Crouch + cover | Crouch behind cover | Cover tier and crouch modifier remain separately inspectable; no accidental double-state overwrite. | RECONSTRUCTION safety |
| COV-009 | Aimed Shot | Fire Aimed Shot through High cover | Ability accuracy modifier participates in hit calculation but does not automatically remove cover. | SOURCE ability + reconstruction |
| COV-010 | Cone per target | Fire shotgun/cone at two targets, only one behind cover | Covered target receives cover resolution; exposed target does not. | RECONSTRUCTION STRONG |
| COV-011 | Attached CC | Use ranged Snare/Disarm ability against covered target | CC applies only if parent attack resolves successfully under current rule. | RECONSTRUCTION STRONG |
| COV-012 | Ground AoE | Explode grenade near covered target | Result remains flagged TEST/UNRESOLVED until AoE occlusion policy is implemented. | UNRESOLVED |
| COV-013 | Cover Defense buff | Activate Hunker Down/cover-defense modifier in cover | Cover-defense modifier changes resolver input without changing visual tier. | CONFIRMED concept / formula unresolved |
| COV-014 | NPC reposition | NPC moves from High cover to exposed position | Cover state updates immediately and subsequent attacks use NONE. | USER-CONFIRMED symmetry + reconstruction |
| COV-015 | Weapon switch | Switch F1–F4 while remaining in cover | Cover state persists independently of active weapon. | SOURCE-BACKED subsystems / reconstruction integration |

## 30_Cover_AI_Tuning

| AI Behavior | Trigger | Decision Input | Default Reconstruction Rule | Evidence Status | col6 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Find cover | NPC under ranged threat | Query nearby valid cover candidates | Prefer valid cover over exposed position according to AI role | USER-CONFIRMED requirement / reconstruction |  |
| Evaluate cover tier | Multiple candidates | Rank available LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH candidates | Tier may influence preference, but exact scoring weights remain tunable | SOURCE-BACKED tiers / reconstruction |  |
| Evaluate direction | Candidate does not face attacker | Reject or strongly penalize candidate | Directional cover is essential | SOURCE-BACKED direction |  |
| Use crouch | At cover or exposed | AI may crouch when behavior profile allows | Crouch is separate from cover quality | SOURCE-BACKED behavior / reconstruction |  |
| Reposition when flanked | Current cover no longer protects from attacker | Seek new cover or engage according to role | Do not keep stale HIGH status after flank | RECONSTRUCTION STRONG |  |
| Suppressed behavior | Suppression active | Prefer hold/reduced aggression/reposition according to final CC semantics | Exact AI effect unresolved | UNRESOLVED |  |
| Aggressive role | Close-range/assault NPC | May leave cover deliberately | Cover should inform AI, not hard-lock every NPC | RECONSTRUCTION |  |
| Boss/special role | Boss or scripted encounter | Behavior may override generic cover selection | Encounter-specific rules can override general AI | RECONSTRUCTION |  |
